Macaulay2 » Documentation
Packages » VectorGraphics :: matrixPlot
next | previous | forward | backward | up | index | toc

matrixPlot -- Graphical representation of a matrix

Description

Represents a matrix or table of real numbers as a black and white rectangle.

i1 : random (RR^6,RR^10)

o1 = | .892712  .89189  .0741835 .741046 .830833 .191734  .557119 .96518 
     | .673395  .231053 .808694  .108386 .538554 .403215  .873708 .681683
     | .29398   .461944 .362835  .348931 .873665 .615911  .7037   .914199
     | .632944  .775187 .706096  .562428 .415912 .0147867 .681869 .887381
     | .0258884 .909047 .127435  .246268 .606588 .223028  .276259 .169813
     | .714827  .314897 .254482  .153346 .848005 .388829  .605659 .965004
     ------------------------------------------------------------------------
     .0647412 .174853 |
     .877846  .626892 |
     .0340514 .350611 |
     .507989  .379495 |
     .150294  .237252 |
     .656391  .116721 |

                6         10
o1 : Matrix RR    <-- RR
              53        53
 
i2 : matrixPlot oo
